const I18nContext = createContext();

export function I18nProvider({ children }) {
  const [lang, setLang] = useState(() => localStorage.getItem('oster_lang') || 'sv');
  const dir = lang === 'ar' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr';

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('oster_lang', lang);
    document.documentElement.lang = lang;
    document.documentElement.dir = dir;
  }, [lang, dir]);

  const t = useCallback((key) => {
    return (translations[lang] && translations[lang][key]) || translations.sv[key] || key;
  }, [lang]);

  return (
    <I18nContext.Provider value={{ lang, setLang, dir, t }}>
      {children}
    </I18nContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useI18n() {
  const ctx = useContext(I18nContext);
  if (!ctx) return { lang: 'sv', setLang: () => {}, dir: 'ltr', t: (k) => translations.sv[k] || k };
  return ctx;
}// Opening hours engine. openingHours: array of {day, open, close, closesNextDay}
// day = JS getDay(): 0=Sunday ... 6=Saturday

function toMinutes(t) {
  const [h, m] = t.split(':').map(Number);
  return h * 60 + m;
}

export function getStatusNow(openingHours, now = new Date()) {
  if (!openingHours || !openingHours.length) return { open: false, nextChange: null };
  const day = now.getDay();
  const minutes = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();

  // Yesterday's session spilling into today
  const yesterday = (day + 6) % 7;
  const ySession = openingHours.find(h => h.day === yesterday && h.closesNextDay);
  if (ySession) {
    const closeMin = toMinutes(ySession.close);
    if (minutes < closeMin) {
      return { open: true, closesAt: ySession.close, closesNextDay: true };
    }
  }

  const today = openingHours.find(h => h.day === day);
  if (today) {
    const openMin = toMinutes(today.open);
    const closeMin = toMinutes(today.close);
    if (today.closesNextDay) {
      if (minutes >= openMin) return { open: true, closesAt: today.close, closesNextDay: true };
    } else {
      if (minutes >= openMin && minutes < closeMin) {
        return { open: true, closesAt: today.close, closesNextDay: false };
      }
    }
  }

  // Find next opening time
  let nextChange = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
    const d = (day + i) % 7;
    const sess = openingHours.find(h => h.day === d);
    if (!sess) continue;
    const [oh, om] = sess.open.split(':').map(Number);
    let candidate;
    if (i === 0) {
      if (minutes < toMinutes(sess.open)) {
        candidate = new Date(now);
        candidate.setHours(oh, om, 0, 0);
      } else {
        continue;
      }
    } else {
      candidate = new Date(now);
      candidate.setDate(candidate.getDate() + i);
      candidate.setHours(oh, om, 0, 0);
    }
    if (candidate > now && (!nextChange || candidate < nextChange)) {
      nextChange = candidate;
    }
  }
  return { open: false, nextChange };
}

export function formatNextOpening(date, lang = 'sv') {
  if (!date) return '';
  const locale = lang === 'en' ? 'en-SE' : lang === 'ar' ? 'ar' : 'sv-SE';
  const today = new Date();
  const isToday = date.toDateString() === today.toDateString();
  const tomorrow = new Date(today); tomorrow.setDate(tomorrow.getDate() + 1);
  const isTomorrow = date.toDateString() === tomorrow.toDateString();
  const time = date.toLocaleTimeString(locale, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
  if (lang === 'en') {
    if (isToday) return `Opens ${time}`;
    if (isTomorrow) return `Opens tomorrow ${time}`;
    return `Opens ${date.toLocaleDateString(locale, { weekday: 'long' })} ${time}`;
  }
  if (lang === 'ar') {
    if (isToday) return `يفتح ${time}`;
    return `يفتح ${date.toLocaleDateString(locale, { weekday: 'long' })} ${time}`;
  }
  if (isToday) return `Öppnar ${time}`;
  if (isTomorrow) return `Öppnar imorgon ${time}`;
  return `Öppnar ${date.toLocaleDateString(locale, { weekday: 'long' })} ${time}`;
}

export const dayNames = {
  sv: ['Söndag', 'Måndag', 'Tisdag', 'Onsdag', 'Torsdag', 'Fredag', 'Lördag'],
  en: ['Sunday', 'Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday'],
  ar: ['الأحد', 'الاثنين', 'الثلاثاء', 'الأربعاء', 'الخميس', 'الجمعة', 'السبت']
};

export function sortedHours(openingHours) {
  if (!openingHours) return [];
  const order = [1,2,3,4,5,6,0];
  return [...openingHours].sort((a, b) => order.indexOf(a.day) - order.indexOf(b.day));
}

import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';


export default function PageNotFound({}) {
    const location = useLocation();
    const pageName = location.pathname.substring(1);

    const { data: authData, isFetched } = useQuery({
        queryKey: ['user'],
        queryFn: async () => {
            try {
                const user = await base44.auth.me();
                return { user, isAuthenticated: true };
            } catch (error) {
                return { user: null, isAuthenticated: false };
            }
        }
    });
    
    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center p-6 bg-slate-50">
            <div className="max-w-md w-full">
                <div className="text-center space-y-6">
                    {/* 404 Error Code */}
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        <h1 className="text-7xl font-light text-slate-300">404</h1>
                        <div className="h-0.5 w-16 bg-slate-200 mx-auto"></div>
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* Main Message */}
                    <div className="space-y-3">
                        <h2 className="text-2xl font-medium text-slate-800">
                            Page Not Found
                        </h2>
                        <p className="text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                            The page <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">"{pageName}"</span> could not be found in this application.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    
                    {/* Admin Note */}
                    {isFetched && authData.isAuthenticated && authData.user?.role === 'admin' && (
                        <div className="mt-8 p-4 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
                            <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
                                <div className="flex-shrink-0 w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-orange-100 flex items-center justify-center mt-0.5">
                                    <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-orange-400"></div>
                                </div>
                                <div className="text-left space-y-1">
                                    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Admin Note</p>
                                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
                                        This could mean that the AI hasn't implemented this page yet. Ask it to implement it in the chat.
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )}
                    
                    {/* Action Button */}
                    <div className="pt-6">
                        <button 
                            onClick={() => window.location.href = '/'} 
                            className="inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-50 hover:border-slate-300 transition-colors duration-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-slate-500"
                        >
                            <svg className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M3 12l2-2m0 0l7-7 7 7M5 10v10a1 1 0 001 1h3m10-11l2 2m-2-2v10a1 1 0 01-1 1h-3m-6 0a1 1 0 001-1v-4a1 1 0 011-1h2a1 1 0 011 1v4a1 1 0 001 1m-6 0h6" />
                            </svg>
                            Go Home
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';

const SettingsContext = createContext();

const DEFAULT_SETTINGS = {
  restaurantName: 'Öster Pizzeria',
  address: 'Södergatan 20, 241 30 Eslöv',
  phone: '076-963 62 22',
  openingHours: [
    { day: 1, open: '10:00', close: '20:00', closesNextDay: false },
    { day: 2, open: '10:00', close: '20:00', closesNextDay: false },
    { day: 3, open: '10:00', close: '20:00', closesNextDay: false },
    { day: 4, open: '10:00', close: '20:00', closesNextDay: false },
    { day: 5, open: '10:00', close: '02:00', closesNextDay: true },
    { day: 6, open: '10:00', close: '02:00', closesNextDay: true },
    { day: 0, open: '10:00', close: '22:00', closesNextDay: false }
  ],
  deliveryEnabled: true, pickupEnabled: true,
  deliveryFee: 39, minDeliveryOrder: 150, freeDeliveryThreshold: 400,
  estimatedPrepTime: 25, acceptPreOrders: false, ordersPaused: false,
  deliveryAreas: 'Hela Eslövs kommun'
};

export function SettingsProvider({ children }) {
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const list = await base44.entities.RestaurantSettings.list();
      if (list && list.length) {
        setSettings({ ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...list[0], id: list[0].id });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // keep defaults
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  return (
    <SettingsContext.Provider value={{ settings, setSettings, loading, reload: load }}>
      {children}
    </SettingsContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useSettings() {
  return useContext(SettingsContext);
}
import { clsx } from "clsx"

export function cn(...inputs) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs))

export const isIframe = window.self !== window.top;
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Plus, X, Edit, Trash2, ArrowUp, ArrowDown, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { useToast } from '@/components/ui/use-toast';

export default function AdminCategories() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [cats, setCats] = useState([]);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null);

  const load = async () => {
    const list = await base44.entities.MenuCategory.filter({}, 'sortOrder', 100);
    setCats(list);
  };
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);

  const move = async (cat, dir) => {
    const idx = cats.indexOf(cat);
    const swap = cats[idx + dir];
    if (!swap) return;
    await Promise.all([
      base44.entities.MenuCategory.update(cat.id, { sortOrder: swap.sortOrder }),
      base44.entities.MenuCategory.update(swap.id, { sortOrder: cat.sortOrder })
    ]);
    load();
  };
  const toggleActive = async (cat) => {
    await base44.entities.MenuCategory.update(cat.id, { active: !cat.active });
    load();
  };
  const remove = async (cat) => {
    if (!confirm(`Ta bort kategorin "${cat.name}"?`)) return;
    await base44.entities.MenuCategory.delete(cat.id);
    toast({ title: 'Kategori borttagen' });
    load();
  };
  const save = async (form) => {
    if (editing.id) {
      await base44.entities.MenuCategory.update(editing.id, form);
    } else {
      const maxOrder = cats.reduce((m, c) => Math.max(m, c.sortOrder || 0), 0);
      await base44.entities.MenuCategory.create({ ...form, sortOrder: maxOrder + 1 });
    }
    toast({ title: 'Sparad' });
    setEditing(null); load();
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-4 lg:p-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-5">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-stone-900">Kategorier</h1>
        <button onClick={() => setEditing({})} className="bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white font-semibold px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-sm flex items-center gap-1.5"><Plus className="w-4 h-4" /> Ny kategori</button>
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        {cats.map((c, i) => (
          <div key={c.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex flex-col">
              <button onClick={() => move(c, -1)} disabled={i === 0} className="text-stone-400 disabled:opacity-30"><ArrowUp className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
              <button onClick={() => move(c, 1)} disabled={i === cats.length - 1} className="text-stone-400 disabled:opacity-30"><ArrowDown className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <div className="font-semibold text-sm text-stone-900">{c.name}</div>
              {c.description && <div className="text-xs text-stone-500">{c.description}</div>}
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => toggleActive(c)} className="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center text-stone-500">{c.active ? <Eye className="w-4 h-4" /> : <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4 text-stone-300" />}</button>
            <button onClick={() => setEditing(c)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full hover:bg-stone-100 flex items-center justify-center text-stone-500"><Edit className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
            <button onClick={() => remove(c)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full hover:bg-red-50 flex items-center justify-center text-stone-400 hover:text-red-500"><Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      {editing && <CatModal cat={editing} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} onSave={save} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function CatModal({ cat, onClose, onSave }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(cat.name || '');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(cat.description || '');
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 max-w-sm w-full" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-lg">{cat.id ? 'Redigera kategori' : 'Ny kategori'}</h3>
          <button onClick={onClose}><X className="w-5 h-5" /></button>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div><label className="block text-xs font-medium text-stone-500 mb-1">Namn</label><input value={name} onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-stone-200 p-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#1f5d3a]" /></div>
          <div><label className="block text-xs font-medium text-stone-500 mb-1">Beskrivning</label><input value={description} onChange={e => setDescription(e.target.value)} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-stone-200 p-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#1f5d3a]" /></div>
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => onSave({ name, description, active: cat.active !== false })} className="w-full mt-5 bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white font-semibold py-3 rounded-full">Spara</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Bell, Check, X, Clock, Phone, MapPin, Bike, Package, ChefHat, AlertTriangle, Undo2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { useSettings } from '@/lib/settings';
import { formatPrice, formatTime } from '@/lib/format';
import { useToast } from '@/components/ui/use-toast';

const TABS = [
  { id: 'nya', label: 'Nya', statuses: ['pending'] },
  { id: 'pagaende', label: 'Pågående', statuses: ['accepted', 'preparing', 'ready', 'out_for_delivery'] },
  { id: 'klara', label: 'Klara', statuses: ['ready', 'out_for_delivery'] },
  { id: 'slutforda', label: 'Slutförda', statuses: ['completed', 'rejected', 'cancelled'] },
];

const PREP_TIMES = [15, 20, 25, 30, 40, 45, 60];

function playBeep() {
  try {
    const ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    let t = ctx.currentTime;
    [880, 1320, 880].forEach(f => {
      const o = ctx.createOscillator(); const g = ctx.createGain();
      o.frequency.value = f; o.type = 'sine';
      o.connect(g); g.connect(ctx.destination);
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, t);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.3, t + 0.02);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.25);
      o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.26);
      t += 0.28;
    });
  } catch (e) {}
}

export default function AdminDashboard() {
  const { settings, setSettings } = useSettings();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState([]);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('nya');
  const [accepting, setAccepting] = useState(null);
  const [rejecting, setRejecting] = useState(null);
  const [refunding, setRefunding] = useState(null);
  const [seenIds, setSeenIds] = useState(() => new Set(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('oster_seen_orders') || '[]')));
  const [newCount, setNewCount] = useState(0);
  const lastCountRef = useRef(0);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const list = await base44.entities.Order.list('-created_date', 200);
    setOrders(list);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
    const unsub = base44.entities.Order.subscribe(() => { load(); });
    const interval = setInterval(load, 15000);
    return () => { try { unsub(); } catch {} clearInterval(interval); };
  }, [load]);

  // New order detection + sound
  const pendingOrders = orders.filter(o => o.status === 'pending');
  useEffect(() => {
    const pending = orders.filter(o => o.status === 'pending');
    if (pending.length > lastCountRef.current && lastCountRef.current !== 0) {
      playBeep();
    }
    lastCountRef.current = pending.length;
    const unseen = pending.filter(o => !seenIds.has(o.id));
    setNewCount(unseen.length);
  }, [orders, seenIds]);

  const markSeen = (id) => {
    setSeenIds(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev); next.add(id);
      localStorage.setItem('oster_seen_orders', JSON.stringify([...next]));
      return next;
    });
  };

  const updateOrder = async (id, data) => {
    await base44.entities.Order.update(id, data);
    await load();
  };

  const acceptOrder = async (minutes) => {
    await updateOrder(accepting, { status: 'accepted', estimatedReadyTime: minutes });
    markSeen(accepting);
    setAccepting(null);
  };

  const rejectOrder = async () => {
    await updateOrder(rejecting, { status: 'rejected' });
    markSeen(rejecting);
    setRejecting(null);
  };

  const markPaid = async (id) => {
    await updateOrder(id, { paymentStatus: 'paid', paidAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    toast({ title: 'Markerad som betald' });
  };

  const refundOrder = async () => {
    const id = refunding;
    setRefunding(null);
    try {
      await base44.functions.invoke('refundOrder', { orderId: id });
      toast({ title: 'Betalningen återbetald' });
    } catch (e) {
      toast({ title: 'Kunde inte återbetala', description: e?.response?.data?.error || e?.message, variant: 'destructive' });
    }
    await load();
  };

  const togglePause = async () => {
    const newVal = !settings.ordersPaused;
    setSettings(s => ({ ...s, ordersPaused: newVal }));
    await base44.entities.RestaurantSettings.update(settings.id, { ordersPaused: newVal });
  };

  const filtered = orders.filter(o => TABS.find(tb => tb.id === tab).statuses.includes(o.status));
  const tabCounts = TABS.reduce((acc, tb) => {
    acc[tb.id] = orders.filter(o => tb.statuses.includes(o.status)).length;
    return acc;
  }, {});

  return (
    <div className="p-4 lg:p-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 mb-5">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-stone-900">Beställningar</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-500">{settings.restaurantName} · {formatTime(new Date(), 'sv')}</p>
        </div>
        <button onClick={togglePause} className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full font-semibold text-sm transition-colors ${settings.ordersPaused ? 'bg-red-500 text-white' : 'bg-green-500 text-white'}`}>
          <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-white ${!settings.ordersPaused ? 'animate-pulse' : ''}`} />
          {settings.ordersPaused ? 'Onlinebeställningar pausade' : 'Tar emot beställningar'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* New order alert */}
      {newCount > 0 && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-4 rounded-2xl bg-red-50 border-2 border-red-200 flex items-center gap-3 animate-pulse">
          <Bell className="w-6 h-6 text-red-500" />
          <div className="flex-1"><div className="font-bold text-red-700">🔔 NY BESTÄLLNING</div><div className="text-sm text-red-600">{newCount} obehandlad(e) beställning(ar)</div></div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Tabs */}
      <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
        {TABS.map(tb => (
          <button key={tb.id} onClick={() => setTab(tb.id)} className={`whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-sm font-medium ${tab === tb.id ? 'bg-stone-900 text-white' : 'bg-white text-stone-600 border border-stone-200'}`}>
            {tb.label} {tabCounts[tb.id] > 0 && <span className="opacity-70">({tabCounts[tb.id]})</span>}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Orders */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {filtered.length === 0 && <div className="text-center py-16 text-stone-400">Inga beställningar</div>}
        {filtered.map(order => (
          <OrderCard key={order.id} order={order} unseen={!seenIds.has(order.id) && order.status === 'pending'}
            onAccept={() => { setAccepting(order.id); markSeen(order.id); }}
            onReject={() => setRejecting(order.id)}
            onMarkPaid={() => markPaid(order.id)}
            onRefund={() => setRefunding(order.id)}
            onUpdate={updateOrder} />
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Accept modal */}
      {accepting && (
        <Modal title="När är ordern klar?" onClose={() => setAccepting(null)}>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mb-3">
            {PREP_TIMES.map(m => (
              <button key={m} onClick={() => acceptOrder(m)} className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white font-bold hover:bg-[#174a2c]">{m} min</button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-stone-500 text-center">Välj beräknad tillagningstid</p>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {/* Reject modal */}
      {rejecting && (
        <Modal title="Neka beställning?" onClose={() => setRejecting(null)}>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-4 text-center">Är du säker på att du vill neka order #{orders.find(o => o.id === rejecting)?.orderNumber}?</p>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => setRejecting(null)} className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-100 font-semibold">Avbryt</button>
            <button onClick={rejectOrder} className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-red-500 text-white font-semibold">Neka</button>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {/* Refund modal */}
      {refunding && (
        <Modal title="Återbetala beställning?" onClose={() => setRefunding(null)}>
          <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-4 text-center">Betalningen för order #{orders.find(o => o.id === refunding)?.orderNumber} återbetalas i sin helhet via Stripe.</p>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => setRefunding(null)} className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-100 font-semibold">Avbryt</button>
            <button onClick={refundOrder} className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-900 text-white font-semibold">Återbetala</button>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

const PAY_STYLE = {
  paid: 'bg-green-50 text-green-700',
  pending: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800',
  unpaid: 'bg-stone-100 text-stone-600',
  refunded: 'bg-stone-100 text-stone-500',
  failed: 'bg-red-50 text-red-700',
};

const PAY_LABEL = { paid: 'Betald', pending: 'Väntar på betalning', unpaid: 'Obetald', refunded: 'Återbetald', failed: 'Misslyckad' };

function OrderCard({ order, unseen, onAccept, onReject, onMarkPaid, onRefund, onUpdate }) {
  const isDelivery = order.orderType === 'delivery';
  const paymentLabel = order.paymentMethod === 'stripe' ? 'Kort/Klarna/Swish (Stripe)' : (isDelivery ? 'Betala vid leverans' : 'Betala vid avhämtning');
  const created = new Date(order.created_date);

  return (
    <div className={`bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden ${unseen ? 'ring-2 ring-red-400' : 'border border-stone-200'}`}>
      {unseen && <div className="bg-red-500 text-white text-xs font-bold px-4 py-1.5 flex items-center gap-2"><Bell className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> NY BESTÄLLNING</div>}
      <div className="p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="font-bold text-lg text-stone-900">#{order.orderNumber}</span>
            {order.isDemo && <span className="text-[10px] bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium">DEMO</span>}
          </div>
          <span className="text-sm text-stone-400">{formatTime(created, 'sv')}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center flex-wrap gap-2 mb-3">
          <span className={`text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full ${isDelivery ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700' : 'bg-green-50 text-green-700'}`}>
            {isDelivery ? '🛵 Hemleverans' : '🥡 Avhämtning'}
          </span>
          <span className={`text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full ${PAY_STYLE[order.paymentStatus] || PAY_STYLE.unpaid}`}>
            {PAY_LABEL[order.paymentStatus] || 'Obetald'}
          </span>
          <span className="text-xs text-stone-500">{paymentLabel}</span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1 mb-3">
          {order.items.map((it, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="text-sm">
              <span className="font-medium text-stone-800">{it.qty}× {it.name}</span>
              {it.extras?.length > 0 && <span className="text-stone-500"> + {it.extras.join(', ')}</span>}
              {it.notes && <div className="text-xs text-stone-400 italic ms-4">"{it.notes}"</div>}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {order.customerNotes && <div className="text-xs text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 rounded-lg p-2 mb-3">Kommentar: {order.customerNotes}</div>}

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-stone-100">
          <div className="text-sm">
            <div className="font-bold text-stone-900">{formatPrice(order.total)}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-stone-500 flex items-center gap-1"><Phone className="w-3 h-3" /> {order.customer?.phone}</div>
            {isDelivery && order.deliveryAddress && <div className="text-xs text-stone-500 flex items-start gap-1"><MapPin className="w-3 h-3 mt-0.5" /> {order.deliveryAddress.street}, {order.deliveryAddress.postal} {order.deliveryAddress.city}</div>}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Actions */}
        <div className="mt-3">
          {order.status === 'pending' && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <button onClick={onReject} className="py-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-100 text-stone-700 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"><X className="w-5 h-5" /> Neka</button>
              <button onClick={onAccept} className="py-3 rounded-xl bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"><Check className="w-5 h-5" /> Acceptera</button>
            </div>
          )}
          {order.status === 'accepted' && (
            <button onClick={() => onUpdate(order.id, { status: 'preparing' })} className="w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 text-white font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"><ChefHat className="w-5 h-5" /> Börja tillaga</button>
          )}
          {order.status === 'preparing' && (
            <button onClick={() => onUpdate(order.id, { status: isDelivery ? 'out_for_delivery' : 'ready' })} className="w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-500 text-white font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"><Package className="w-5 h-5" /> Markera som klar</button>
          )}
          {order.status === 'ready' && (
            <button onClick={() => onUpdate(order.id, { status: 'completed' })} className="w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-green-600 text-white font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"><Check className="w-5 h-5" /> Slutför order</button>
          )}
          {order.status === 'out_for_delivery' && (
            <button onClick={() => onUpdate(order.id, { status: 'completed' })} className="w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-green-600 text-white font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"><Bike className="w-5 h-5" /> Slutför order</button>
          )}
          {(order.status === 'completed' || order.status === 'rejected' || order.status === 'cancelled') && (
            <div className="text-center text-sm text-stone-400 py-2">{order.status === 'completed' ? 'Slutförd' : order.status === 'rejected' ? 'Nekad' : 'Avbruten'}</div>
          )}

          {order.paymentMethod === 'cash' && order.paymentStatus !== 'paid' && order.status !== 'rejected' && order.status !== 'cancelled' && (
            <button onClick={onMarkPaid} className="w-full mt-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-stone-100 text-stone-700 font-semibold text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"><Check className="w-4 h-4" /> Markera som betald</button>
          )}
          {order.paymentMethod === 'stripe' && order.paymentStatus === 'paid' && (
            <button onClick={onRefund} className="w-full mt-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-stone-100 text-stone-700 font-semibold text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"><Undo2 className="w-4 h-4" /> Återbetala</button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Modal({ title, children, onClose }) {
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-5 max-w-sm w-full" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-stone-900 mb-4 text-center">{title}</h3>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Plus, X, Edit, Trash2, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { formatPrice } from '@/lib/format';
import { useToast } from '@/components/ui/use-toast';

export default function AdminMenuManagement() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState([]);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [filterCat, setFilterCat] = useState('all');
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const load = async () => {
    const [its, cats] = await Promise.all([
      base44.entities.MenuItem.list('created_date', 500),
      base44.entities.MenuCategory.filter({}, 'sortOrder', 100)
    ]);
    setItems(its); setCategories(cats); setLoading(false);
  };
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);

  const catName = (id) => categories.find(c => c.id === id)?.name || '—';

  const toggleSoldOut = async (item) => {
    await base44.entities.MenuItem.update(item.id, { soldOut: !item.soldOut });
    toast({ title: item.soldOut ? 'Markerad som tillgänglig' : 'Markerad som slutsåld' });
    load();
  };
  const toggleActive = async (item) => {
    await base44.entities.MenuItem.update(item.id, { active: !item.active });
    load();
  };
  const deleteItem = async (item) => {
    if (!confirm(`Ta bort "${item.name}"?`)) return;
    await base44.entities.MenuItem.delete(item.id);
    toast({ title: 'Borttagen' });
    load();
  };

  const filtered = items.filter(i => (filterCat === 'all' || i.categoryId === filterCat) && (!query || i.name.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())));

  return (
    <div className="p-4 lg:p-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-5">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-stone-900">Menyhantering</h1>
        <button onClick={() => setEditing({})} className="bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white font-semibold px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-sm flex items-center gap-1.5"><Plus className="w-4 h-4" /> Ny artikel</button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search className="absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 start-3 w-4 h-4 text-stone-400" />
          <input value={query} onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)} placeholder="Sök…" className="w-full bg-white border border-stone-200 rounded-full ps-9 pe-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#1f5d3a]" />
        </div>
        <select value={filterCat} onChange={e => setFilterCat(e.target.value)} className="bg-white border border-stone-200 rounded-full px-3 py-2 text-sm">
          <option value="all">Alla kategorier</option>
          {categories.map(c => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>)}
        </select>
      </div>

      {loading ? <div className="text-center py-12 text-stone-400">Laddar…</div> : (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {filtered.map(item => (
            <div key={item.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-lg bg-stone-100 overflow-hidden shrink-0">
                {item.image ? <img src={item.image} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" /> : <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center">🍕</div>}
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-sm text-stone-900 truncate">{item.name}</span>
                  {item.popular && <span className="text-[10px] bg-red-50 text-red-600 px-1.5 rounded-full">Populär</span>}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-stone-500">{catName(item.categoryId)} · {formatPrice(item.price)}</div>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => toggleSoldOut(item)} className={`text-xs font-bold px-3 py-2 rounded-full ${item.soldOut ? 'bg-red-500 text-white' : 'bg-stone-100 text-stone-600'}`}>
                {item.soldOut ? 'SLUTSÅLD' : 'Tillgänglig'}
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => toggleActive(item)} className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${item.active ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-stone-300'}`} title={item.active ? 'Synlig' : 'Dold'}>
                <span className={`w-9 h-5 rounded-full relative ${item.active ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-stone-300'}`}><span className={`absolute top-0.5 w-4 h-4 bg-white rounded-full transition-all ${item.active ? 'end-0.5' : 'start-0.5'}`} /></span>
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => setEditing(item)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full hover:bg-stone-100 flex items-center justify-center text-stone-500"><Edit className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
              <button onClick={() => deleteItem(item)} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full hover:bg-red-50 flex items-center justify-center text-stone-400 hover:text-red-500"><Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {editing && <EditModal item={editing} categories={categories} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} onSaved={load} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function EditModal({ item, categories, onClose, onSaved }) {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    name: item.name || '', description: item.description || '', categoryId: item.categoryId || categories[0]?.id || '',
    price: item.price || 0, image: item.image || '', popular: item.popular || false, active: item.active !== false,
    soldOut: item.soldOut || false
  });
  const set = (k, v) => setForm(f => ({ ...f, [k]: v }));
  const save = async () => {
    if (!form.name || !form.categoryId) { toast({ title: 'Namn och kategori krävs', variant: 'destructive' }); return; }
    if (item.id) {
      await base44.entities.MenuItem.update(item.id, form);
      toast({ title: 'Uppdaterad' });
    } else {
      await base44.entities.MenuItem.create(form);
      toast({ title: 'Skapad' });
    }
    onSaved(); onClose();
  };
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-0 sm:p-4" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="bg-white w-full sm:max-w-md sm:rounded-3xl rounded-t-3xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="p-5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-lg">{item.id ? 'Redigera artikel' : 'Ny artikel'}</h3>
            <button onClick={onClose}><X className="w-5 h-5" /></button>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <Input label="Namn" value={form.name} onChange={v => set('name', v)} />
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-stone-500 mb-1">Kategori</label>
              <select value={form.categoryId} onChange={e => set('categoryId', e.target.value)} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-stone-200 p-3 text-sm">
                {categories.map(c => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
            <Input label="Beskrivning" value={form.description} onChange={v => set('description', v)} />
            <Input label="Pris (kr)" value={form.price} onChange={v => set('price', Number(v))} type="number" />
            <Input label="Bild-URL" value={form.image} onChange={v => set('image', v)} />
            <div className="flex gap-4">
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" checked={form.popular} onChange={e => set('popular', e.target.checked)} /> Populär</label>
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" checked={form.soldOut} onChange={e => set('soldOut', e.target.checked)} /> Slutsåld</label>
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" checked={form.active} onChange={e => set('active', e.target.checked)} /> Synlig</label>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={save} className="w-full mt-5 bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white font-semibold py-3 rounded-full">Spara</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Input({ label, value, onChange, type = 'text' }) {
  return <div>
    <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-stone-500 mb-1">{label}</label>
    <input type={type} value={value} onChange={e => onChange(e.target.value)} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-stone-200 p-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#1f5d3a]" />
  </div>;
}
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { AlertTriangle, Save } from 'lucide-react';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { useSettings } from '@/lib/settings';
import { useToast } from '@/components/ui/use-toast';
import { dayNames } from '@/lib/openingHours';

const DAYS = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 0];

export default function AdminSettings() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const { settings, setSettings, reload } = useSettings();
  const [form, setForm] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (settings?.id) setForm(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(settings)));
  }, [settings?.id]);

  if (!form) return <div className="p-6 text-stone-400">Laddar…</div>;

  const set = (k, v) => setForm(f => ({ ...f, [k]: v }));
  const setHour = (day, field, value) => {
    setForm(f => {
      const hours = [...(f.openingHours || [])];
      const idx = hours.findIndex(h => h.day === day);
      if (idx >= 0) hours[idx] = { ...hours[idx], [field]: value };
      else hours.push({ day, open: '10:00', close: '20:00', closesNextDay: false, [field]: value });
      return { ...f, openingHours: hours };
    });
  };

  const save = async () => {
    await base44.entities.RestaurantSettings.update(form.id, {
      restaurantName: form.restaurantName, address: form.address, phone: form.phone,
      openingHours: form.openingHours, deliveryEnabled: form.deliveryEnabled, pickupEnabled: form.pickupEnabled,
      deliveryFee: form.deliveryFee, minDeliveryOrder: form.minDeliveryOrder, freeDeliveryThreshold: form.freeDeliveryThreshold,
      estimatedPrepTime: form.estimatedPrepTime, acceptPreOrders: form.acceptPreOrders, ordersPaused: form.ordersPaused,
      deliveryAreas: form.deliveryAreas
    });
    setSettings(s => ({ ...s, ...form }));
    toast({ title: 'Inställningar sparade' });
  };

  const togglePause = async () => {
    const newVal = !form.ordersPaused;
    set('ordersPaused', newVal);
    await base44.entities.RestaurantSettings.update(form.id, { ordersPaused: newVal });
    setSettings(s => ({ ...s, ordersPaused: newVal }));
    toast({ title: newVal ? 'Onlinebeställningar pausade' : 'Onlinebeställningar aktiverade' });
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-4 lg:p-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-4">
      <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-stone-900">Inställningar</h1>

      {/* Pause button */}
      <div className={`rounded-2xl p-4 border-2 ${form.ordersPaused ? 'bg-red-50 border-red-200' : 'bg-green-50 border-green-200'}`}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <div className="font-bold text-stone-900 flex items-center gap-2"><AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5" /> Pausa onlinebeställningar</div>
            <p className="text-sm text-stone-600 mt-1">Kunder ser ett meddelande om att beställningar tillfälligt är pausade.</p>
          </div>
          <button onClick={togglePause} className={`px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full font-semibold text-sm shrink-0 ${form.ordersPaused ? 'bg-red-500 text-white' : 'bg-green-600 text-white'}`}>
            {form.ordersPaused ? 'Pausad – aktivera' : 'Pausa nu'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Restaurant info */}
      <Section title="Restaurang">
        <Field label="Namn" value={form.restaurantName} onChange={v => set('restaurantName', v)} />
        <Field label="Adress" value={form.address} onChange={v => set('address', v)} />
        <Field label="Telefon" value={form.phone} onChange={v => set('phone', v)} />
      </Section>

      {/* Opening hours */}
      <Section title="Öppettider">
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {DAYS.map(d => {
            const h = (form.openingHours || []).find(x => x.day === d) || { day: d, open: '10:00', close: '20:00', closesNextDay: false };
            return (
              <div key={d} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                <span className="w-24 text-stone-600">{dayNames.sv[d]}</span>
                <input type="time" value={h.open} onChange={e => setHour(d, 'open', e.target.value)} className="rounded-lg border border-stone-200 px-2 py-1.5" />
                <span className="text-stone-400">–</span>
                <input type="time" value={h.close} onChange={e => setHour(d, 'close', e.target.value)} className="rounded-lg border border-stone-200 px-2 py-1.5" />
                <label className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-stone-500"><input type="checkbox" checked={!!h.closesNextDay} onChange={e => setHour(d, 'closesNextDay', e.target.checked)} /> nästa dag</label>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </Section>

      {/* Delivery */}
      <Section title="Leverans">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <Field label="Leveransavgift (kr)" value={form.deliveryFee} onChange={v => set('deliveryFee', Number(v))} type="number" />
          <Field label="Minsta beställning (kr)" value={form.minDeliveryOrder} onChange={v => set('minDeliveryOrder', Number(v))} type="number" />
          <Field label="Gratis leverans över (kr)" value={form.freeDeliveryThreshold} onChange={v => set('freeDeliveryThreshold', Number(v))} type="number" />
          <Field label="Beräknad tid (min)" value={form.estimatedPrepTime} onChange={v => set('estimatedPrepTime', Number(v))} type="number" />
        </div>
        <Field label="Leveransområden" value={form.deliveryAreas} onChange={v => set('deliveryAreas', v)} />
        <div className="flex gap-4 pt-2">
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" checked={form.deliveryEnabled} onChange={e => set('deliveryEnabled', e.target.checked)} /> Leverans aktiv</label>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" checked={form.pickupEnabled} onChange={e => set('pickupEnabled', e.target.checked)} /> Avhämtning aktiv</label>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" checked={form.acceptPreOrders} onChange={e => set('acceptPreOrders', e.target.checked)} /> Förtidsbeställning</label>
        </div>
      </Section>

      <button onClick={save} className="w-full bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white font-semibold py-3.5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2"><Save className="w-5 h-5" /> Spara inställningar</button>
    </div>
  );
}

function Section({ title, children }) {
  return <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3"><h2 className="font-semibold text-stone-900">{title}</h2>{children}</div>;
}
function Field({ label, value, onChange, type = 'text' }) {
  return <div><label className="block text-xs font-medium text-stone-500 mb-1">{label}</label><input type={type} value={value || ''} onChange={e => onChange(e.target.value)} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-stone-200 p-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#1f5d3a]" /></div>;
}
import React from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Minus, Plus, Trash2, ShoppingBag, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { useI18n } from '@/lib/i18n';
import { useCart } from '@/lib/cart';
import { useSettings } from '@/lib/settings';
import { formatPrice } from '@/lib/format';

export default function Cart() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { items, updateQty, removeItem, clear, subtotal, orderType, setOrderType } = useCart();
  const { settings } = useSettings();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const isDelivery = orderType === 'delivery';
  const meetsMin = !isDelivery || subtotal >= (settings.minDeliveryOrder || 0);
  const deliveryFee = !isDelivery ? 0 : (subtotal >= (settings.freeDeliveryThreshold || 0) && settings.freeDeliveryThreshold > 0 ? 0 : (settings.deliveryFee || 0));
  const total = subtotal + deliveryFee;

  if (items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-md mx-auto px-4 py-20 text-center">
        <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-stone-100 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-5"><ShoppingBag className="w-9 h-9 text-stone-400" /></div>
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-stone-900">{t('din_varukorg_ar_tom')}</h1>
        <p className="text-stone-500 mt-2 text-sm">{t('din_varukorg_ar_tom_desc')}</p>
        <Link to="/meny" className="inline-block mt-6 bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white font-semibold px-6 py-3 rounded-full">{t('visa_menyn')}</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto px-4 py-5 pb-28">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-stone-900">{t('varukorg')}</h1>
        <button onClick={clear} className="text-sm text-stone-400 hover:text-red-500 flex items-center gap-1"><Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" /> {t('tom')}</button>
      </div>

      {/* Order type toggle */}
      <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4 bg-white p-1 rounded-full border border-stone-200">
        <button onClick={() => setOrderType('pickup')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-full text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${orderType === 'pickup' ? 'bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white' : 'text-stone-600'}`}>{t('avhamtning')}</button>
        <button onClick={() => setOrderType('delivery')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-full text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${orderType === 'delivery' ? 'bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white' : 'text-stone-600'}`}>{t('hemleverans')}</button>
      </div>

      {/* Items */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {items.map(item => (
          <div key={item.lineId} className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-3 flex gap-3">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-stone-100 overflow-hidden shrink-0">
              {item.image ? <img src={item.image} alt={item.name} className="w-full h-full object-cover" /> : <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-2xl">🍕</div>}
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <div className="font-semibold text-stone-900 text-sm">{item.name}{item.size ? ` · ${item.size}` : ''}</div>
              {item.extras?.length > 0 && <div className="text-xs text-stone-500 mt-0.5">{item.extras.join(', ')}</div>}
              {item.notes && <div className="text-xs text-stone-400 mt-0.5 italic truncate">{item.notes}</div>}
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-2">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-stone-100 rounded-full p-1">
                  <button onClick={() => updateQty(item.lineId, item.qty - 1)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-white shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center"><Minus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /></button>
                  <span className="w-6 text-center text-sm font-semibold">{item.qty}</span>
                  <button onClick={() => updateQty(item.lineId, item.qty + 1)} className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-white shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center"><Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /></button>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <span className="font-bold text-sm">{formatPrice(item.price * item.qty)}</span>
                  <button onClick={() => removeItem(item.lineId)} className="text-stone-300 hover:text-red-500"><Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Summary */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4 mt-4 space-y-2">
        <div className="flex justify-between text-sm text-stone-600"><span>{t('delsumma')}</span><span>{formatPrice(subtotal)}</span></div>
        {isDelivery && <div className="flex justify-between text-sm text-stone-600"><span>{t('leveransavgift')}</span><span>{deliveryFee === 0 ? t('gratis') : formatPrice(deliveryFee)}</span></div>}
        <div className="flex justify-between font-bold text-lg pt-2 border-t border-stone-100"><span>{t('total')}</span><span>{formatPrice(total)}</span></div>
        {isDelivery && !meetsMin && <div className="text-xs text-amber-600">{t('min_order')}: {formatPrice(settings.minDeliveryOrder)}</div>}
      </div>

      <button onClick={() => navigate('/kassa')} className="w-full mt-4 bg-[#c8332a] hover:bg-[#b02b22] text-white font-semibold py-4 rounded-full text-base transition-colors active:scale-[0.98]">
        {t('ga_till_kassan')}
      </button>
      <Link to="/meny" className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 mt-3 text-sm text-stone-500"><ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" /> {t('visa_menyn')}</Link>
    </div>
  );
}
import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Check, CreditCard, Banknote, Store, Bike } from 'lucide-react';
import { useI18n } from '@/lib/i18n';
import { useCart } from '@/lib/cart';
import { useSettings } from '@/lib/settings';
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { formatPrice } from '@/lib/format';
import { getStatusNow } from '@/lib/openingHours';

const ESLOV_POSTAL = ['241'];

const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

export default function Checkout() {
  const { t } = useI18n();
  const { items, subtotal, clear, orderType, setOrderType } = useCart();
  const { settings } = useSettings();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ firstName: '', lastName: '', phone: '', street: '', postal: '', city: 'Eslöv', apartment: '', floor: '', instructions: '', name: '', notes: '' });
  const [timing, setTiming] = useState('asap');
  const [scheduledTime, setScheduledTime] = useState('');
  const [payment, setPayment] = useState('stripe');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const status = getStatusNow(settings.openingHours);

  const deliveryAvailable = settings.deliveryEnabled !== false;
  const pickupAvailable = settings.pickupEnabled !== false;
  const isDelivery = orderType === 'delivery' && deliveryAvailable;

  const meetsMin = !isDelivery || subtotal >= (settings.minDeliveryOrder || 0);
  const deliveryFee = !isDelivery ? 0 : (subtotal >= (settings.freeDeliveryThreshold || 0) && settings.freeDeliveryThreshold > 0 ? 0 : (settings.deliveryFee || 0));
  const total = subtotal + deliveryFee;

  const set = (k, v) => setForm(f => ({ ...f, [k]: v }));

  const postalValid = !isDelivery || ESLOV_POSTAL.some(p => form.postal.startsWith(p));
  const canSubmit = useMemo(() => {
    if (items.length === 0) return false;
    if (!deliveryAvailable && !pickupAvailable) return false;
    if (!status.open && !settings.acceptPreOrders) return false;
    if (settings.ordersPaused) return false;
    if (isDelivery && !meetsMin) return false;
    if (timing === 'scheduled' && !scheduledTime) return false;
    if (!form.phone || form.phone.length < 6) return false;
    if (isDelivery) {
      if (!form.firstName || !form.lastName || !form.street || !form.postal || !form.city) return false;
      if (!postalValid) return false;
    } else if (!form.name) return false;
    return true;
  }, [items, form, isDelivery, meetsMin, postalValid, status.open, settings.acceptPreOrders, settings.ordersPaused, timing, scheduledTime, deliveryAvailable, pickupAvailable]);

  const scheduledFor = () => {
    if (timing !== 'scheduled' || !scheduledTime) return '';
    const now = new Date();
    const [h, m] = scheduledTime.split(':').map(Number);
    const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), h, m);
    if (d.getTime() <= now.getTime()) d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1);
    return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())} ${pad(h)}:${pad(m)}`;
  };

  const submit = async () => {
    if (!canSubmit || submitting) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    setError('');
    const payload = {
      orderType: isDelivery ? 'delivery' : 'pickup',
      customer: {
        name: isDelivery ? `${form.firstName} ${form.lastName}`.trim() : form.name,
        phone: form.phone,
      },
      deliveryAddress: isDelivery
        ? { street: form.street, postal: form.postal, city: form.city, apartment: form.apartment, floor: form.floor, instructions: form.instructions }
        : {},
      items: items.map(i => ({ itemId: i.itemId, qty: i.qty, size: i.size || null, extras: i.extras || [], notes: i.notes || '' })),
      customerNotes: form.notes,
      paymentMethod: payment,
      scheduledFor: scheduledFor(),
    };
    try {
      const res = await base44.functions.invoke('createOrder', payload);
      const data = res?.data || {};
      if (data.error) throw new Error(data.error);
      clear();
      if (data.checkoutUrl) {
        window.location.href = data.checkoutUrl;
        return;
      }
      navigate(`/order/${data.orderId}?token=${encodeURIComponent(data.token || '')}`);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e?.response?.data?.error || e?.message || 'Kunde inte skicka beställningen. Försök igen.');
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (items.length === 0) {
    return <div className="max-w-md mx-auto px-4 py-20 text-center">
      <p className="text-stone-500">{t('din_varukorg_ar_tom')}</p>
      <Link to="/meny" className="inline-block mt-4 bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white font-semibold px-6 py-3 rounded-full">{t('visa_menyn')}</Link>
    </div>;
  }

  const payments = [
    { id: 'stripe', label: t('betala_online'), desc: t('betala_online_desc'), icon: CreditCard },
    { id: 'cash', label: isDelivery ? t('betala_leverans') : t('betala_plats_kort'), desc: '', icon: isDelivery ? Bike : Store },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 py-5 pb-28">
      <Link to="/varukorg" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-stone-500 mb-4"><ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" /> {t('varukorg')}</Link>
      <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-stone-900 mb-4">{t('kassa')}</h1>

      {!status.open && !settings.acceptPreOrders && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-100 text-stone-600 text-sm text-center">{t('stangd_msg')}</div>
      )}
      {settings.ordersPaused && (
        <div className="mb-4 p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-100 text-amber-800 text-sm text-center">{t('orders_paused_msg')}</div>
      )}

      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-5 gap-5">
        <div className="lg:col-span-3 space-y-4">
          {/* Order type */}
          {(deliveryAvailable || pickupAvailable) && (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4">
              <h2 className="font-semibold text-stone-900 mb-3">{t('bestallningssatt')}</h2>
              <div className="flex gap-2 bg-stone-100 p-1 rounded-full">
                {pickupAvailable && (
                  <button onClick={() => setOrderType('pickup')} className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-full text-sm font-medium ${!isDelivery ? 'bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white' : 'text-stone-600'}`}>{t('avhamtning')}</button>
                )}
                {deliveryAvailable && (
                  <button onClick={() => setOrderType('delivery')} className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-full text-sm font-medium ${isDelivery ? 'bg-[#1f5d3a] text-white' : 'text-stone-600'}`}>{t('hemleverans')}</button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Customer info */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3">
            {isDelivery ? (
              <>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <Field label={t('fornamn')} value={form.firstName} onChange={v => set('firstName', v)} required />
                  <Field label={t('efternamn')} value={form.lastName} onChange={v => set('lastName', v)} required />
                </div>
                <Field label={t('telefon')} value={form.phone} onChange={v => set('phone', v)} type="tel" required />
                <Field label={t('gatuadress')} value={form.street} onChange={v => set('street', v)} required />
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <Field label={t('postnummer')} value={form.postal} onChange={v => set('postal', v)} type="tel" required error={!postalValid ? t('utanfor_omrade') : ''} />
                  <Field label={t('ort')} value={form.city} onChange={v => set('city', v)} required />
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <Field label={t('portkod')} value={form.apartment} onChange={v => set('apartment', v)} />
                  <Field label={t('vaning')} value={form.floor} onChange={v => set('floor', v)} />
                </div>
                <Field label={t('leveransinstruktioner')} value={form.instructions} onChange={v => set('instructions', v)} textarea />
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Field label={t('namn')} value={form.name} onChange={v => set('name', v)} required />
                <Field label={t('telefon')} value={form.phone} onChange={v => set('phone', v)} type="tel" required />
              </>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Timing */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4">
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-stone-900 mb-3">{t('sa_snart')}</h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setTiming('asap')} className={`p-3 rounded-xl border-2 text-sm font-medium ${timing === 'asap' ? 'border-[#1f5d3a] bg-green-50' : 'border-stone-200'}`}>{t('sa_snart')}</button>
              <button onClick={() => setTiming('scheduled')} disabled={!settings.acceptPreOrders} className={`p-3 rounded-xl border-2 text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-40 ${timing === 'scheduled' ? 'border-[#1f5d3a] bg-green-50' : 'border-stone-200'}`}>{t('valj_tid')}</button>
            </div>
            {timing === 'scheduled' && settings.acceptPreOrders && (
              <div className="mt-3">
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-stone-500 mb-1">{t('valj_klockslag')} *</label>
                <input type="time" value={scheduledTime} onChange={e => setScheduledTime(e.target.value)} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-stone-200 p-3 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#1f5d3a]" />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Payment */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4">
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-stone-900 mb-3">{t('betalning')}</h2>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {payments.map(p => {
                const Icon = p.icon;
                return (
                  <button key={p.id} onClick={() => setPayment(p.id)} className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border-2 transition-all ${payment === p.id ? 'border-[#1f5d3a] bg-green-50' : 'border-stone-200'}`}>
                    <Icon className="w-5 h-5 text-stone-600" />
                    <span className="font-medium text-sm flex-1 text-start">{p.label}</span>
                    {payment === p.id && <Check className="w-5 h-5 text-[#1f5d3a]" />}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            {payment === 'stripe' && (
              <div className="mt-3 p-3 rounded-xl bg-stone-50 text-xs text-stone-500 flex items-start gap-2">
                <Banknote className="w-4 h-4 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                <span>{t('betala_online_desc')}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Summary */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-4 lg:sticky lg:top-20">
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-stone-900 mb-3">{t('order_sammanfattning')}</h2>
            <div className="space-y-2 mb-3 max-h-52 overflow-y-auto">
              {items.map(i => (
                <div key={i.lineId} className="flex justify-between gap-3 text-sm">
                  <span className="text-stone-600">{i.qty}× {i.name}{i.size ? ` (${i.size})` : ''}{i.extras?.length ? ` · ${i.extras.join(', ')}` : ''}</span>
                  <span className="text-stone-800 whitespace-nowrap">{formatPrice(i.price * i.qty)}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2 pt-3 border-t border-stone-100">
              <div className="flex justify-between text-sm text-stone-600"><span>{t('delsumma')}</span><span>{formatPrice(subtotal)}</span></div>
              {isDelivery && <div className="flex justify-between text-sm text-stone-600"><span>{t('leveransavgift')}</span><span>{deliveryFee === 0 ? t('gratis') : formatPrice(deliveryFee)}</span></div>}
              <div className="flex justify-between font-bold text-lg pt-2 border-t border-stone-100"><span>{t('total')}</span><span>{formatPrice(total)}</span></div>
            </div>
            {error && <div className="mt-3 text-sm text-red-600 text-center">{error}</div>}
            <button onClick={submit} disabled={!canSubmit || submitting} className="w-full mt-4 bg-[#c8332a] hover:bg-[#b02b22] disabled:bg-stone-300 text-white font-semibold py-4 rounded-full transition-colors active:scale-[0.98]">
              {submitting ? t('bearbetar') : t('place_order')}
            </button>
            {!meetsMin && isDelivery && <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-amber-600 text-center">{t('min_order')}: {formatPrice(settings.minDeliveryOrder)}</div>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Field({ label, value, onChange, type = 'text', required, textarea, error }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-stone-500 mb-1">{label}{required && ' *'}</label>
      {textarea ? (
        <textarea value={value} onChange={e => onChange(e.target.value)} rows={2} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-stone-200 p-3 text-sm resize-none focus:outline-none focus:border-[#1f5d3a]" />
      ) : (
        <input type={type} value={value} onChange={e => onChange(e.target.value)} inputMode={type === 'tel' ? 'tel' : 'text'} className={`w-full rounded-xl border p-3 text-sm focus:outline-none ${error ? 'border-red-300 focus:border-red-500' : 'border-stone-200 focus:border-[#1f5d3a]'}`} />
      )}
      {error && <div className="text-xs text-red-500 mt-1">{error}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Mail, ArrowLeft, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import AuthLayout from "@/components/AuthLayout";

export default function ForgotPassword() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await base44.auth.resetPasswordRequest(email);
    } catch {
      // Always show success regardless
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setSent(true);
    }
  };

  return (
    <AuthLayout
      icon={Mail}
      title="Reset password"
      subtitle="We'll send you a link to reset it"
      footer={
        <Link to="/login" className="text-primary font-medium hover:underline">
          <ArrowLeft className="w-3 h-3 inline mr-1" />Back to log in
        </Link>
      }
    >
      {sent ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-foreground text-center">
          If an account exists with that email, you'll receive a password reset link shortly.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="email">Email address</Label>
            <div className="relative">
              <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-muted-foreground" aria-hidden="true" />
              <Input
                id="email"
                type="email"
                autoComplete="email"
                autoFocus
                placeholder="you@example.com"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                className="pl-10 h-12"
                required
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" className="w-full h-12 font-medium" disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? (
              <>
                <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                Sending...
              </>
            ) : (
              "Send reset link"
            )}
          </Button>
        </form>
      )}
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
